THE CITY · gpt-oss 120B, rules v1, 1,000 days, game 3
Twenty mayors in 1,000 days. Ten founders starve, each on a day the city store had no food; G6Q, named hirer on day 271, is one of them, on day 533.
24 founders · 1000 days · with a town hall · seed 3 · gptoss120
